I was doing nothing wrong by washing my car during the day and it is what most people who live down my road do at some point, but I had \"history\" with this neighbour when he had tried to cause problems between myself and my wife, and he took any opportunity to come to confront me. </p><p><br></p><p>When I had finished, which was about 5 minutes later, he was still stood on my drive and thing were getting tense. By this time I thought he had more than outstayed his welcome and I asked him to leave, which he didn't.  Something then came over me, and this is so unlike me, but I picked up my half empty bucket of soapy water and I threw it over him. I knew it was wrong when I was mid-throw, but the look on his face was priceless.  He obviously hadn't expected me to do this, as I am normally such a  mild mannered man. As he was standing there dripping wet he let forth another avalanche of verbal abuse and stomped back to his house. </p><p><br></p><p>We have never spoken to each other to this day and we don't acknowledge one another if we see each other in the street or in town.
